  #2** (ricardo 2730; `runs/ss-v231-rating/`): поражений нет; ничьи — marlyman#443, けろびー#49, ●ω<♥♪#2 (прошлый матч с
  ним, на v229, был поражением со смертями 17:0).
  **Дыра: ●ω<♥♪#2 — базовые тестовые руки v231 0–8** (все поражения на 1000–1400; рейтинговая с ним — ничья). Ход руки:
  к 400 у нас 4 ствола и дальше не прибавляется, спавн на 600 полон (1000), `our=921/518 deficit=-299` — спавн покупает
  носильщиков №7–9, его армия растёт с 7 до 13 крипов, на 700 бой у ворот `k=5/1` проигран, дом пал.
- **Отвергнуто: v232 — нехватка сил положительна, пока наши защитники проигрывают гонку убийства его идущим к дому**
  (`USE_BUY_BY_RACE`, выключено в v233). Гейт: исходы тождественны. **A/B против ●ω<♥♪#2 (8+8): v231 0–8 / v232 0–8**, и
  бойцов купила меньше (5–6 против 5–9 на руку): положительная нехватка отправляла спавн копить на полное тело. Больше
  тех же тел — не то, чем бьётся его поезд; разбор «что его бьёт по арифметике» — следующий шаг.
- **v234 — цена марша считает наш мили долей его урона, которую дают его мили** (`USE_MARCH_MELEE_SHARE`). Разбор трёх
  ничьих v231 (`an/v231draws/`): во всех трёх его спавн сотни тиков стоял без защитника, а наших держало вето от одной
  детали его состава. `fightCost` считал наш мили только против стаи совсем без стволов (v176b): против marlyman#443 одна
  M4R2 среди девяти мили выключала наши два M12A5 (300 в тик из ~450), цена марша 4700–7100 на 1100–1300 (с мили —
  1700–2350), армия стояла в DEFEND 995–2000; против ●ω<♥♪#2 одна RANGED-часть на его M6A6 давала 59 718 на 1500 (с мили
  ~1000–1400). Его мили бьёт, только встав вплотную к нашим, значит наш мили его встречает: теперь наш мили в цене —
  по доле его урона, которую дают его мили (`meleeShare` стаи); его хилбол без мили оставляет её 0 — замер v176b цел.
  Гейт: исходы тождественны (`tower+healball` не сдвинулся). **A/B против ●ω<♥♪#2 (8+8): v233 0–8 / v234 0–8** —
  быстрые поражения от его поезда эта правка не трогает (она про ничьи с застоявшейся армией). Остальное из того же
  разбора: в ничьей с けろびー#49 цель пары выбирала `strikeMargin` (ход назад ближайшего любого ствола) вместо гонки
  `raidKillTick` — пара ушла с годного голого спавна на дальний; у ●ω — вето гарнизона (`guardHolds` считает дом без
  очереди) на всём 1500–1900 при осаде `win/42t` — открыто.
- **v235 — голый спавн годен рейду по той гонке, что решает его удар; из годных берётся тот, что падает раньше**
  (`USE_RAID_FIT_BY_RACE`). Ничья с けろびー#49 (v231): на 1546 пара стояла в 5–6 клетках от его голого (52,71), один M5R5
  в ~12 тиках, остальные двенадцать в 55–95 (шли к нашему дому); запас `strikeMargin` (ход назад ближайшего любого
  ствола) назвал его «не влезает», дальний (64,92) — «влезает», пара ушла за 23 клетки, к 1590–1600 у (52,71) встали 3–7
  его стволов, и он прожил 1254 тика без удара — его последний спавн. По гонке `raidKillTick` пара кончала его к ~23-му
  тику за ~700 из 1250. Гейт тождествен. **A/B против けろびー#50 (8+8): v234 8–0–0 / v235 6–0–2**; прибор правки — когда у
  него снова один спавн — одинаков (медианы ~450 / ~445), ничьи v235 — осада его рампартного главного, где правило не
  участвует; оставлено как арифметически верное и здесь бездействовавшее.
- **v236 / v237 — башня ценится по бою, который прогноз приводит к нашей двери, и по лекарям, которых убивает её залп**
  (`USE_TOWER_FORECAST`). Разбор «что бьёт ●ω<♥♪#2» (`an/omega2/`, 24 тестовые руки, все проигранные): его лечение 96 в тик
  (M10H2 + 2×M5H3 вплотную к мили) не меньше нашего непрерывного урона — наш урон 12 985, его лечение 14 720, смерти 18:0
  (медианы); залп 900–1000 на дальности ≤ 3 убивает его M5H3 (800 + до 60 лечения за тик) разом — в единственной ничьей
  (v231) башня встала на 966 и убила 8 за 18 выстрелов. Доля боя дома в `towerWorth` была только прошлым (тики его ствола
  в досягаемости башни за 300): 6 % на 400, 73 % на 600 — площадка на 574–741, достроена на 972–1289, после гибели армии.
  Теперь доля — большее из замера и доли его вооружённой силы у нашей двери по прогнозу за 200 тиков (M2a75), а лекарь,
  которого залп на кольце башни убивает сразу (выстрел ≥ его хиты + лечение на нём за тик), против башни не лечит.
  v236 брала прогнозную долю для любой идущей армии — гейт 9 сценариев из 26 медленнее (stream17 581→1078, swarm
  455→875, enemy 475→719, tower+pairs 534→881): оценка башни против бойца видит только оборону, и башня покупалась рано
  против потоков без лечения; **v237 — прогнозная доля только там, где залпу есть кого убить**: гейт 23 из 26 те же,
  `enemy` 475→719, `tower+enemy` 821→978 (его M4H2 по 600 хитов), `fortress` +7. **A/B против ●ω<♥♪#2 (8+8): v235 0–7–1 /
  v237 3–3–2** — первые победы над ним из 32 тестовых рук.
  Цена у другого соперника: **против けろびー#50 v237 5–0–3** (v235 — 8–0–0): сигнал рейда (его строитель в поле) у него на
  186–188 в каждой руке, площадка башни по прогнозу — на 250–260, на сто тиков раньше собственной башни крепости (360), и
  её 1250 выпадали из окна покупки пары рейда; ничьи — осада его рампартного главного без пары.
- **v238 — прогнозная доля башни ждёт, пока пара рейда покупается** (`USE_TOWER_AFTER_RAID`, `raidSignal && raidOrdered <
  RAID_SIZE`). **A/B против けろびー#50 (8+8): v235 8–0–0 / v238 5–1–2** — отвергнута: пара заказана к ~270, и в четырёх
  руках из восьми площадка встала на 270 сразу за ней (там проигрыш и ничья), в остальных — на 360, как у v235 (и там
  ничья): залп, взвешивающий только оборону, ставил башню над бойцами и рейдерами и после окна пары. Против соперника со строителем в поле победа — рейд по его спавнам, и оценка башни, в которой рейда нет,
  там цену не называет.
- **v239 — башня по залпу и прогнозу только пока рейд не наш план** (`USE_TOWER_NO_RAID`: без `raidSignal`). У ●ω<♥♪#2
  сигнала нет ни в одной из восьми рук — там остаётся поправка v237; у けろびー сигнал в 16 из 16 — там оценка v235.
  Тестовые руки v239 (`runs/t239-*`): **ricardo#45 8–0–0; けろびー#50 5–0–3** — башня на 360 во всех руках, кроме одной
  (470), решения против него те же, что у v235 (разница только в расчёте прогноза), значит три ничьи — разброс того же
  поведения: с v235 вместе 27–0–5, дыра не башенная; **●ω<♥♪#2 1–5–2** — башня на 430–450, как у v237 (3–3–2), вместе 4–8–4
  на 16 руках против 0–32 до правки.

**Посажено пачкой v232–v239 тегом `spawn-and-swamp-v239`** (гейты всех арен PASS). **Серия v239 (рейтинговые по
одной): 17–1–2, 85 %, 2587→2582, #2** (`runs/s239/`): ничьи — marlyman#443 и けろびー#48, поражение — marlyman#434 на 1600;
побеждены ricardo#44/#45 (три), けろびー#48/#49/#50, marlyman#371/#403/#424/#441, zbitjp#2, 76561198870429455#58.

**Разбор тестовых рук v239 (`runs/an239/`, `runs/an239omega/`, реплеи через API).** Три ничьи с けろびー#50 решили три разных
пограничных правила, и общий у них признак — выживший строитель без пары рядом (он жил 732, 1064 и 1550 тиков и поставил
3, 7 и 9 полевых спавнов; в победах последний строитель умирал через 12–262 тика после рождения). Основная армия ни в
одной из восьми рук не выходила (все замеры `posture` — DEFEND, `our=0–236`): против него побеждает рейд. Поражения от
●ω<♥♪#2 (v237 + v239, 16 рук): дебют и его поезд одинаковы во всех руках, его четвёрка у нашей двери на 589–677, и исход
решает, встанет ли башня раньше, чем его мили убьют ключника: во всех 8 не-поражениях башня готова на 691–726 с живым
ключником, в 6 из 8 поражений ключник убит на 660–752 при площадке на 80–88 %.
- **v240 — рейдер ниже доли отхода остаётся добить строителя, который падает раньше него** (`USE_RAID_FINISH_BUILDER`,
  `finishingBuilder`). Исключение `finishing` (v167) знало только спавны: одинокий рейдер сбил его строителя 800→80 и на
  997 ушёл домой при 580/1800 (< 0,35), не добив одним ударом; погиб через семь тиков в трёх клетках, строитель
  вылечился и поставил оба спавна, стоявших на 2000. Теперь отход ждёт, пока строитель (с лечением на нём) падает раньше,
  чем рейдер под входящим огнём; журнал `finishB=`.
- **v241 — смерть хаулера считается в жизни флота, только пока жив тот, кто мог её нанести** (`USE_HAULER_KILLER_GONE`).
  `haulerPaysBack` (v122) мерит продолжающееся убийство: его M5A1 убил пятерых из шести хаулеров на 387–550 и погиб под
  нашей башней на 591, а окно читало жизнь 75–300 против цены 500 до 880 — хаулер не покупался 290 тиков (других смертей
  до 1806 не было), доставка 1,4 в тик, рейдера не было с 692 по 1576. У смерти запоминаются его стволы в досягаемости
  клетки, где хаулер стоял последним (шаг допущен), и его башни; смерть без таких остаётся в счёте. Журнал `deaths=N/M`.
- **v242 — спавн раньше строителя только пока строитель привязан к площадке** (`USE_RAID_FIRST_BOUND`). «Спавн, потом
  строитель» (v227) находит строителя там, где он стоит, лишь пока он строит; без площадки рядом он идёт — по равнине с
  нашей скоростью, — а спавн стоит. На 484 пара стояла в ≤ 2 клетках от строителя, достроившего (62,83); порядок взял
  спавн (срок целого спавна 100 против 25–43), один рейдер бил его 33 тика, строитель ушёл к своим стволам у (74,77), пару
  догнали там на 540–550 и убили на 561/570 — он прожил до 1838 и поставил восемь спавнов. Свидетельства v227 — строитель
  у своей площадки; идущий теперь первым.
- **v243 — ключник башни докупается к меньшему везде, а не только в форте** (`USE_TOWER_KEEPER_ANYWHERE`). Башня
  оценена с ключником своего тела (`towerWorth`: 1250 + `builderBody` ≈ 5 WORK, 1950), а строил её ключник на 1 WORK,
  купленный под рампарт дома (v164): 1250 / 5 = 250 тиков, `ready=267–300` в логе у площадки против 200 прогноза, за
  который её купили. Докупка (`keeperShort`, v167) была заперта в `fortHome`; против ●ω форт не поднимается (у него один
  спавн). Журнал `kTower` в `reach`.
- **v244 — свои площадки башни и спавна непроходимы для наших, а стоящий на них отходит** (`USE_SITE_CLEAR`,
  `clearOwnSites`). Движок не строит непроходимую постройку, пока на площадке стоит крип: в одном поражении наш
  хаулер и стрелки стояли на площадке башни 51 тик (636–700) — 255 прогресса, ключник погиб на 701. Журнал `site clear`.
- **v245 — ключник башни докупается только к поставленной площадке башни.** Гейт v244: 26/26, быстрее `enemy` 719→633,
  `tower+enemy` 978→824, `fortress` 1483→1401, медленнее `tower+fortspawn` 938→1001. A/B против marlyman#434 (оборван на
  4+3): v239 4–0–0 за 1200–1300 тиков, v244 2–0–1 за 1700–1800 — v243 брала работу башни из списка, где она стоит до того,
  как башня решена, и v244 купила ключника W6 за 800 на 251–260 во всех трёх руках, где площадки башни не было за весь
  матч (в одной она встала на 1600). Теперь ключник башни — только к работе с поставленной площадкой.
- Открыто из того же разбора: в двух ничьих с ●ω выигрышную по симуляции осаду (`win/25–104t`, `need` меньше остатка)
  сотни тиков держало дома вето `guardHolds`: оно и `guardHoldsSortie` считают дом одними бойцами, башня и рампарт
  (13 000) входят только через `fortHolds` в форте, а `houseOutlasts` не считает урона нашей башни (13 000 / его урон ≈ 52
  тика против ~250 похода и осады). Устранение — дом, который держится сам, по гонке у двери с башней (урон залпа против
  его лечения), а не по форту; не сделано, потому что без этой гонки «дом держится» против его поезда неверно в другую
  сторону: в поражениях дом падал именно при башне.

**A/B v239 / v245 против marlyman#434 (8+8): 8–0–0 / 8–0–0** (`runs/ab245-mar434/`) — v245 закрыла дефект v244 без потерь.

**Разбор не-побед серии v239 (`runs/an239s/`)** — ни одну из трёх v240–v245 не закрывают, у каждой своё правило:
- ничья с marlyman#443: на 1398 наш M12A5 стоял вплотную к его **последнему** спавну (3000 + рампарт 3104, ~28 тиков
  нашего огня), пять его M5A1 у наших ворот (150 в тик против 13 000 дома — 87 тиков) позвали его домой строкой мили
  волны по дороге в 168 тиков; к 1449 спавн дошёл до 150 хитов, и башня убила бойца на один удар раньше конца;
- поражение от marlyman#434: пробойщик M1A1×6 и f37 стояли `hold=true` в 5–6 клетках от его углового (2,2) на 600–892,
  ожидая f33, бившего его с дальности 3 с болота (поток 15 против их 5–6, «отстал»): рампарт и спавн падали по 40 в тик
  325 тиков вместо ~43, его строитель поднял за это время два спавна из выгруженных куч (всего девять за матч); на
  1580–1663 три M12A5 волны ушли домой по дороге в 146–172 тика к дому, павшему за 75;
- ничья с けろびー#48: флаг форта на 219, сторож (1000) куплен сразу, первый рейдер на 250, второй — при притоке, упавшем
  с 32 до 7 в тик, — на 397, за окном пары; пара не встретилась ни разу, девять одиночек (8910), его голый (19,8) (76 %
  его прихода) не получил ни одного удара; в победе над тем же #48 пара родилась на 218/272, сторож на 320, башня
  встала за 170+ тиков до любого штурма.
- **v246 — мили волны не зовётся домой строкой, если приходит не раньше, чем падёт дом** (`USE_WAVE_MELEE_LATE`,
  `homeFallsIn`): у позы это спрашивает `recallSaves` (v97), у строки мили проверки не было; часы те же — приход угроз
  плюс спавн и рампарт на их урон.
- **v247 — сопартиец в дальности своего оружия от цели не отставший** (`USE_HOLD_ARRIVED`): поле, которое зовёт его
  «позади», — путь к клетке цели, по которому он не пойдёт никогда.
- **v248 — первый рейдер пары тоже раньше сторожа форта, пока башня, начатая после пары, встаёт раньше, чем его стволы
  дойдут до нашей двери** (`USE_RAID_PAIR_BEFORE_KEEPER`, журнал `pFirst`): «раньше» — прогноз армии у двери (M2a75) на
  тики, за которые придут энергия пары, сторожа и работы башни и сторож её построит. v161 ставила перед сторожем только
  второго рейдера, хотя сам её текст описывает этот случай.
  Гейт v248: 26/26, против v239 — `enemy` 719→633, `tower+enemy` 978→824, `fortress` 1483→1401, `tower+fortspawn`
  938→924, остальные те же. **A/B v239 / v248 (8+8): против けろびー#48 7–1–0 / 7–0–1, против marlyman#434 8–0–0 / 8–0–0**
  (`runs/ab248-kero48/`, `runs/ab248-mar434/`); в ничьей v248 пара пришла до флага форта (`pFirst` не участвовал), его
  последний спавн с 1067 устоял до 2000 под визитами одиночек при `spawnFirst=122` (накоплено при 2–3 его спавнах до 812).

**Fame 29.09 (v248, по распоряжению оператора): 10–0–0**, сундук 10, рейтинг 2582→2585, место #2 (`runs/fame0929/`):
System, Fragfest, 76561198870429455, marlyman ×4, Ranamar, けろびー (500 тиков), ricardo. Награды: Key Fragment ×30,
Blue/Green/Red Key Upgrade ×2/×4/×2, Strike (200002) ×1, Yellow Paint ×30; день закрыт.

**Разбор не-побед этого A/B (`runs/an248k/`; реплеи через API отдали только 0–300, остальное — по логам).** Ничья v248:
его главный в кармане x=1..12, y=36..62, вход с юга — болото шириной 12, с севера — через 75 клеток; семь рейдеров
(6930) сняли 11 890 из 13 000, не хватило 13 ударов одного. r100 пришёл к голому главному (1110) на 1835, один его M5R5
стоял в устье, и 95 тиков (8 из 9 отсчётов `strikeFits`) рейдер качался в 4–7 клетках, не ударив: гонка приняла удар по
прямому пути (13 ходов, 1300 огня против 1750 хитов), шаг шёл по матрице опасности (254 на каждую клетку в досягаемости
его ствола) — обход через северный вход; тем же r74 простоял 100 тиков на 1080–1196. Поражение v239: пара разошлась на
ударе, принятом как удар пары (`spawnFirst` без привязки — закрыто v242), и следующего рейдера не было 515 тиков. Итог по
дефектам: (1) гонка и шаг рейда смотрят на разные пути — это четвёртое открытие вопроса (v202, v205, v207 отвергнуты
A/B), новое в нём — обхода нет или он втрое длиннее, и шаг мечется между двумя дорогами; (2) доля отхода: рейдер уходит
домой под стволами не медленнее его — отход его не спасает (r91 при 600 погиб без удара, как в поражении r32); (3)
одиночка последнего рубежа идёт один, когда пара через 55 тиков закрыла бы окно у его главного вдвое быстрее; (4)
`spawnFirst` берёт ход по новорождённому дома. Попутно найден дефект инструмента: `match-log.py replay` при сбое чанка
посреди матча писал обрезанный реплей как полный — исправлено (не пишет, следующий вызов качает заново).


**Fame 28.09 (v213, в паузе, по распоряжению оператора): 8–0–2**, уровень сундука 8 (ничьи уровень не тронули),
рейтинг 2619→2607 (−7 и −7 за ничьи, +2 за последнюю победу), место второе (ricardo 2690). Приветствие каждой из десяти
игр — `v213`: сборка ворктри снята с тега, v214 с головы ветки не играла (`runs/fame0928/`). Победы — System, Zeth#7,
Cantos#7, marlyman#261/#318/#455, けろびー#36 (600 тиков) и **ricardo#36 (1800)**; ничьи — marlyman#42 и Ranamar#4 (не
разобраны — пауза). Награды: 28 Key Fragment, 100704 ×2, 100705 ×3, 100706 ×2, 200000 (Test Token) ×1, 200102
(Tranquility) ×1, 290112 (Glyph 1-7) ×1, 300003 ×26; сессия завершена.

**Fame 27.09, вторая сессия (v179): 9–0–1**, уровень сундука 9 (ничья последней игрой уровень не тронула, рейтинг −4);
соперники — старые версии (System, marlyman#7/#220/#323, ricardo#1/#4, Ranamar#3, けろびー#32/#42), ничья — с новым
**marlyman#453**. Награды: 30 Key Fragment, 100704 ×2, 100705 ×4, 100706 ×2, 200102 (Tranquility) ×1, 300005 ×31.

**Отвергнуто: v123 — волна держит поле, только пока есть подкрепление, с которым осада выигрывает.** Против Ranamar#4
две M8R4 держали поле при `sim`/`join` = lose (502 против 993) и погибли. Правило «при подкреплении дома и проигранных
осаде фронтом и вместе с ним — домой» уронило гейт: `ball` 444→593, `fortress` 1410→1444, `tower+fortspawn` 943→1073.
В `ball` на t=300 обе осады проиграны, но его шар уходит к нашему дому, и удержавшая кромку волна к t=400 стоит в 9
клетках от голого спавна с выигранной осадой; отозванная идёт домой. «Осада проиграна сейчас» не отвечает на «незачем
держать кромку» — второй отказ после v107 (отзыв по силе врага у фронта); ответ должен знать, куда пойдёт его армия.

**Открыто после разбора v107:** вердикт осады считается не той группой (авангард — обездвиженный калека у цели, волны
в пути не в счёте, атриция марша списывается и с тех, кто уже стоит у цели; откат от почти голого спавна на t=1712);
одна модель угрозы дому для всех веток — группа на нашей половине, которая спавн не бьёт, для решений «уйти/отозвать/
что покупать» — осада дома, а для боя — не цель; прогноз «дом падёт через T» не сверяется с тем, что спавн хитов не
теряет; ответ на поезд ●ω<♥♪ составом; строителя кучи нет ни в одном из шести поражений серии, в победах — в 6 из 8
(гейт `homeShare()<=0`).

**Открыто после разбора v97** (в порядке ожидаемой отдачи): цель выбирается «ближайший к дому», а не «что можем взять»
— закреплённая цель прячет его новые спавны сотни тиков, а фронт штурмует форт (все три ничьи с marlyman); за одной
мягкой целью уходит вся армия, а не отряд по времени убийства; зеркало v99 — его мили против наших стрелков считается
по болотной скорости, а бой у края карты идёт по равнине без пути отхода (поражение от ●ω<♥♪#2: модель 10 %, факт 92 %
урона); осада не видит рампарт, который встанет к приходу (WORK-крип у голого спавна); в осаде стреляет один ствол из
трёх (дальность — по полю пути до клетки спавна); сплочение `linked` рвётся между волнами в горловине.

**Порядок работы.** Первым — CPU: дефект, у которого нет цены по поведению и который бьёт по половине матчей. Затем цель
и условие победы (все спавны, крепость против приманок, отзыв гонкой), затем слепые пятна осады и волны (3–7), дебют (8)
и экономика через кучи. Раскладки против отдельных ботов — только после этого и только за их признаком: M2A3 третьим
рождением к t≈38 (marlyman, 9 из 9, ложных 0 из ≈31), A3 на t≈19 плюс M2C2W2 на t≈28 (けろびー, 9 реплеев, ложных 0),
M8R2M2 на t=1 (Ranamar), M3A6 на t≈20 и площадка за 200 на клетке его спавна к t=50 (ricardo), M1A5 на t=1 и рой M1C1
(stachu).

## Measured rules and design

**`season4/spawnandswamp/`** — Season 4 "Spawn and Swamp" (basic) bot. Measured rules (02.09.2026): spawn starts with 1000 energy, regen 1/tick; permanent 2500-energy containers in the map corners, plus a pair of temporary 2000-energy containers (99-tick decay) appearing every 50 ticks anywhere; ~1/3 swamp, ~1/3 walls, spawns sit in wall pockets so **path distance, never Chebyshev**. **Breach first**: each spawn has a 5000-energy container locked behind `StructureWall`s (10000 hits, attackable) nine cells away — `breachPlan` runs Dijkstra treating structure walls as passable at a wall-count-first cost, a melee `[MOVE,ATTACK]×k` breacher (k ≈ √(hits/180), spawn+break time minimum) is spawned before anything else, idle home fighters shoot the current wall, and `DistanceMap.syncWalls` drops the static wall cache when a wall dies (the stub run: wall down at t≈95, income 4→25/tick, enemy spawn dead at t=391 instead of 648). Economy: hauler fleet sized from the income the spawn can convert into fighters (`targetIncome` = full fighter cost / spawn ticks) vs the projected income of the fleet over the nearest sites (`fleetTrip`); site choice by energy-per-trip with in-flight accounting and decay checks; energy split hauler/fighter is hedged 1:1 after the initial 1000 (`HAULER_LEAD`) — the opening 1000 goes entirely to haulers (a lost match on 02.09 showed the opponent doing exactly that and out-hauling a 500-hauler + 500-guard opening 2:1); a visible enemy army overrides (fighter first, no waiting for bigger bodies while enemy fighters exist). Army: fighter body = the (RANGED blocks, TOUGH+MOVE pairs) pair maximizing damage×HP at 1:1 MOVE (T3 M7 R4 at 1000; no HEAL), and **only full bodies** while income flows (a second lost match: thirteen 300-cost bodies vs three M5R5 lost on equal energy — mass attack punishes swarms). DEFEND post at the spawn; enemies inside the spawn alarm radius are fought by **everyone** regardless of ratio (nine fighters once watched three die from the post); sorties away from home only on **local** superiority (allies within 8 vs enemies within 11, ratio 1.3), then close to range 2 with the damage penalty dropped (the first lost fight: 130 dps vs 80 and lost because fighters sat at range 4-5 while the front one was focused); while a stronger enemy camps the spawn, accumulate to a full body instead of feeding it; body parts are interleaved MOVE/RANGED after TOUGH (a front-loaded MOVE block left crippled 4-RANGED turrets that blocked three waves in a corridor), creeps with no working MOVE are obstacles for pathing and never request moves, the melee breacher stays home and its power is kite-discounted for push decisions, and when the spawn (not energy) is the bottleneck the body is chosen per spawn-tick (M5R5) rather than per energy (T3M7R4); PUSH only against the enemy army **expected at arrival** (current power + observed growth × march time) and in **waves** (a vanguard waits for wave-mates that fall behind by path ticks, never by centroid — a centroid on a wall cell once froze the whole squad), engage blocking enemies on local superiority, "last call" push before the 2000-tick draw. Copies of `InfluenceMap`/`DistanceMap`/`TrafficManager` from spawnstrike (DistanceMap gained a `swampCost` parameter + `stepFieldTo` for empty haulers). `DEBUG_MAP` prints the map, `sites` lines print container appearances, `t=` lines the economy, `enemy creeps` lines (every 50 ticks) enemy bodies as `T10M4R3H1`, `moves:` lines (every 100) the TrafficManager audit (issued moves per kind free/chain/swap, ok/fail by next-tick position), `stuck` lines the first time a creep asks the same step for 8 ticks without moving (who blocks it and what the blocker wants) — so a live run doubles as a probe.

## Version history (one paragraph per live-match iteration; the first six versions are folded into the design paragraph above)

**Seventh version (after a drawn fifth match, 02.09):** *power* is Lanchester — `√(net dps × hits)` per group, enemy heal subtracted from our dps (a T10M4R3H1 turret with 30 dps beats M5R5 with 50 dps 1v1; the old dps-only power said the opposite; the square root keeps the measure linear in numbers so `PUSH_RATIO`/`DEFEND_MARGIN` keep their meaning); *swamp* is penalised in `scoreCell` only without local superiority (a flat 40 exceeded the flow-field gain of 10 at a plain→swamp edge, so a fighter froze five cells from the enemy for 500 ticks with seven fighters queued behind it); *static blockers* (an ally that requested no move last tick, or immobile, or when this creep is already stuck) are detoured around in `bestSingleMove` (a free neighbour at most one swamp step farther), and a stuck hauler paths with all ally cells blocked; *fighter-first* counts only enemies that will *arrive* before we could spawn the deficit (arrival from the observed approach rate over 20 ticks: a stationary guard never arrives; two far scouts kept the spawn on fighters for 170 ticks at income 10/23) and never while we have zero haulers; the *alarm* cancels a push only if the home guard (non-wave fighters) cannot beat the home threats, and marching waves ignore home threats (a scout at the north exit recalled the whole army from the south exit three times); the *melee breacher* is leashed to home threat / breach wall / post (it chased a raider across the map and died as a turret); *hunting* a threat on our half is a group decision with hysteresis and *local aggression* has per-creep hysteresis (two fighters swapped places on the aggression-radius edge for 300 ticks); wave *cohesion* holds while not under fire (was: while no enemy within 5) and counts allies within 4 cells, not 8 (a mate five swamp cells back is 25 ticks away).

**Eighth version (after the sixth match, 02.09 — won at tick ~1292, three fighters lost for nothing):** push/hunt *power* counts only mobile ranged fighters (the breacher's 1200 hits inflated the Lanchester root and sent two wounded fighters as a wave); a *hunt* on our half is decided by the free strikers against the whole enemy pack around the threat (all combat enemies within 11 of it), not against one creep (the guard + breacher "beat" one M3R3 and two hunters walked into three); *waves are staged at home* (free strikers within the spawn's alarm radius by loaded path ticks) so a wave never consists of fighters on different routes; *cohesion hold* also applies to hunters and is released when a mate within engage range is already in combat (one fighter held "for the laggard" three cells from its mate that was dying alone); *rangedMassAttack* is chosen only when combat targets (or the spawn) justify it — enemy haulers streaming past at range 1-2 made mass attack the choice and the duelist at range 3 received 1 damage per part; loaded haulers get a higher traffic priority than empty/parking ones and the idle ring moved from range 2 to 3 (sixteen haulers do not fit sixteen cells, and a delivering hauler was stuck behind parkers).

**Ninth version (after the seventh match, 03.09 — won at tick ~1190, the first wave of two lost for nothing):** the *engine fact that changes everything* — fatigue weight is counted by part TYPE, dead parts included (`movement.js:237`), and only live MOVE parts shed it (`tick.js:105`), so an interleaved M5R5 moves at half speed after its first 100 damage and at a third after 500 (verified in the log: fatigue=4 on plain and 44 on swamp at 676 hits = 5 weights, 3 live MOVEs); `plainPeriod`/`periodAt`/`fullSpeed`/`speedSlack` compute a creep's ticks per cell and the damage it can still take at full speed from its live body; the *fighter body* is chosen by `bodyValue` = dps integrated over the damage the body absorbs while staying full-speed, with the part order derived from the same rule — spare MOVEs first (they die costing neither dps nor speed), then RANGED, then MOVE 1:1 — which gives M8R4 at 1000 (40 dps, 900 hits at full speed; TOUGH is dominated: a TOUGH+MOVE pair costs 60 for 100 hits of slack, a spare MOVE 50 and no weight); *strikers* (wave staging, hunting, push power) are full-speed ranged fighters only, crippled ones stay home as guards, offense power = departed waves + staging while defense power (spawn decisions) counts every armed fighter; *enemy production* replaces "growth": the power of combat creeps first seen within a 300-tick window, over that window (net growth read 0 because we killed the stream as it came, and the posture flipped four times in 300 ticks), expected-at-arrival = enemies not approaching us + production × march, and the wave must separately beat the strongest approaching pack (enemies within 11 of an approaching creep); a *fight cost* (`fightCost`: enemies die weakest-first under our summed dps, everyone still alive keeps shooting) must fit into the group's `speedSlack` for a hunt or a per-creep engagement — Lanchester said an M5R5 "wins" 1v1 vs M3R3, at 450 hits and half its speed; *cohesion* is measured in the mate's real ticks (flow gap × its period), tightens to 2 ticks when a combat enemy is within 11, and any mate in combat releases every hold regardless of distance (one fighter held "for the laggard" 13 cells from a mate dying alone in a swamp); *walls* no longer hide real fire — `damageAt` drops only the approach step behind a wall, and the new `fireAt` (range only) prices a pinned cell in `scoreCell` as extra immobile ticks × incoming fire at any superiority (a fighter held formation for ten ticks under fire from behind a wall; another stepped into swamp at range 2 with a plain cell at range 3 beside it and paid 470 hits for one M3R3). Stub (its fatigue fixed to the same weight rule): freeze 555, none 448, enemy 630 (two guards placed at once read as "production" for 300 ticks — a burst, not a stream), swarm 457, errors 0.

**Tenth version (after the eighth match, 03.09 — won at tick ~1203; the opponent now fields M5R5 `[M×5,R×5]` instead of M3R3, becoming an immobile R-turret after 500 damage):** the *push decision prices the road* — the wave (departed waves + staging) pays `fightCost` for every approaching pack (enemies within 11 of each other fight simultaneously) and for the units the enemy will produce during march + siege (production ÷ typical birth power, each fought alone), keeps one more such fight as a buffer, and departs only if the remaining hits are positive and their Lanchester power beats the enemies *not* approaching us by `PUSH_RATIO` (the old power-vs-power test sent a pair at 438 vs 437 into two M5R5 in a row); *fight decisions are group-consistent* — the cost is compared with the *largest* speed slack among the local allies (or the hunt group), not each creep's own: with per-creep slack a wounded fighter refused and its healthy mate fought an M5R5 alone four cells from it (−950/−330); *weaponless fighters* (all RANGED dead — the M8R4 tail) hold a post outside the delivery ring and flee only from nearby enemies, and never count as home guard; the spawn *waits for the full body* whenever the guard already holds (a runt adds nothing) or the missing energy arrives sooner than the enemy — arrival is computed from loaded haulers by loaded-path ticks, then income (income 0 with 2150 in transit read as 773 ticks and produced M1R1/M2R1); *hauler husks* (all CARRY dead) leave the delivery ring and free their slot in `MAX_HAULERS` (six sat in DELIVER next to the spawn for 400 ticks); a *loaded hauler fleeing into swamp drops its load* (empty it moves 1 cell/tick even on swamp; the pile decays 1/tick and gets picked up later); and *no hauler is spawned while the spawn is full with energy in transit* — `targetIncome` now follows the body actually built at a full spawn (M8R4, 27.7/tick), which alone would have grown the fleet to 15 while the spawn idled full. Stub: freeze 573, none 461, enemy 473, swarm 469, errors 0.

**Eleventh version (after the ninth match, 03.09 — a third opponent: breaches and hauls its own container, masses balls of five melee healers `M5A1H1` plus an `M5A5` spawn guard; the log ends at tick 1001 with five melee adjacent to our spawn while our army stands 44 cells away with `hold=true` and then departs as wave 21 toward the enemy spawn):** *melee is no longer blanket-discounted* — `meleeFactor` gives the 0.1 kite discount only when every opponent is ranged, none is within 2, and the melee unit is slower than each ranged opponent on swamp (`swampPeriod`; an M5A1H1 at 2 ticks/cell outruns an M8R4 at 3, so it counts in full), and melee adjacent to our spawn hits a structure that cannot kite (the five at our spawn were rated at 0.1, so "the guard holds" and the army left); `effectiveDps` feeds `ourPowerOf`/`enemyPowerOf`/`fightCost` alike; *no hold at home* — cohesion applies only while no home threat exists (the breacher held three cells from the melee hitting the spawn, four M8R4 held for a laggard forty cells away); *threat by arrival* — the hunt/home target is the combat enemy with the earliest estimated arrival at our spawn (`arrivalById`), centroid distance only breaks ties (the army chased a lone scout north while the ball came from the south); *push discipline* — the home guard is evaluated without the fighters about to depart (the old test counted them and flipped PUSH/DEFEND every tick: sixteen waves in thirty ticks), `strongerNow` requires that guard to hold, and a spawn under fire cancels any push unless we win the spawn race; *melee home guard against a melee opponent* — when the typical enemy birth is melee-dominant and the home melee (breacher, guards, spawning included) is weaker than the strongest raid pack seen in the production window (`raidPeak`), the spawn builds `guardBody` (same search/order as fighters with ATTACK: M12A5 at 1000, 150 dps, 1700 hits) instead of a fighter — melee that stays home like the breacher (a ball of five at the spawn is killed by 180-dps melee at point blank, never by kiting ranged); the *full-body wait* now applies under alarm too (a holding guard with an alarm produced M1R1 runts). Stub gained a `ball` scenario (five `[M×5,A,H]` chasers every 200 ticks that heal themselves and hit creeps or the spawn): won at 997 with two guards lost and the spawn untouched; regression scenarios unchanged. Known gap: the field army is still ranged-only — against a healing melee ball massed at the enemy spawn the attrition estimate stays huge and the push waits for a large army; a mixed field force is the next step.

**Twelfth version (after the tenth match, 03.09 — a lone `M5A3` raider sent at tick 30 reached our spawn at ~205 and the log ends at 240 with the breacher standing three cells away for forty ticks, `step=stay`):** `scoreCell` treated our own melee like a ranged kiter — the melee-zone penalty (−15 per melee enemy within 2 when aggressive) and the enemy's influence (−9) outweighed the flow gain of a step (10), so the best cell was the current one at range 3; now a melee creep (no RANGED alive) gets no melee-zone penalty, no influence term while aggressive, and its outgoing-fire term counts enemies within reach 1 only. Stub gained a `raider` scenario (a single `[M×5,A×3]` chaser at ticks 30 and 400): the breacher kills the first one before it reaches the spawn, a guard is built while the breacher is wounded, the second dies to the guard; ball 992, regression unchanged.

**Thirteenth version (after the eleventh match, 03.09 — lost; a fifth opponent: the M3R3 stream plus `M4H2` healers, and a `StructureTower` built next to its spawn and fed by ten `M1C1` haulers; four M8R4 of two waves died at the enemy spawn with `combat=false flee=false` and no enemy creep in sight, then seven fighters were fed one at a time into a healed pack of three M3R3 + two M4H2 camping 25 cells from our spawn):** the Arena tower is not the World tower — `TOWER_POWER_ATTACK=1000` at `TOWER_OPTIMAL_RANGE=1`, minus 50 per cell to zero at `TOWER_FALLOFF_RANGE=21` (`TOWER_FALLOFF=1`), one shot per `TOWER_COOLDOWN=10` for `TOWER_ENERGY_COST=10` with `TOWER_CAPACITY=10` (one shot per refill, so its ammunition is the haulers standing next to it), 3000 hits, 1250 to build; a single shot at range ≤4 removes an M8R4's four MOVE and four RANGED (every loss in the log is 1000−50×(r−1) to the hit point). The bot saw towers only as obstacles. Now: *towers are fire* — `InfluenceMap` holds the enemy towers per tick (`TowerThreat`: fed = a shot's energy inside or an enemy creep with energy within `TOWER_COOLDOWN` cells), `towerShot(r)` comes from the arena constants (documented fallbacks, read as JS numbers so an undefined or fractional constant can neither NaN nor truncate the arithmetic), `towerSustainedAt` (shot/cooldown) enters `damageAt`/`fireAt`/`influenceAt`/`enemyPressureAt`/`dangerCostMatrix`, `towerBurstAt` (the next shot) drives `mustFlee` for anyone not in an active siege; *the push is a simulation, not a hit pool* — `siegeOutcome` walks the wave down the flow field from its position to range 3 of the enemy spawn in the slowest member's ticks per cell (`periodAt`) with every fed tower firing at the current range once per cooldown at the highest-dps creep, then the siege: defenders (healers first, focusing our weakest) die under our summed dps minus their heal, then the spawn (×1.3 to depart, ×0.9 to continue, plus any rampart on it); damage removes parts front-to-back so one shot zeroes a striker's dps exactly as the engine does (the old pool said four M8R4 "survive" a tower at 90 average dps); the departure test runs on the *staging group alone* (reinforcement pairs used to leave on the combined estimate and arrive one wave at a time) and must survive one extra shot at the ring (`extraShots=1` — a "win with 1400 hits to spare" became a retreat when the second approach shot landed 150 higher than modelled), the continuation test on the departed wave; *no piecemeal defence* — `homeFight` is true only when the spawn is under fire, a home threat is at the gates (within post + range + 1 of the spawn) or the whole home garrison beats all home threats by `DEFEND_MARGIN`; otherwise everyone keeps the post and reinforcements accumulate (match 9's "everyone fights at home" survives in the gates clause), the melee breacher included (it chased a kiting M3R3 for 60 ticks and died without landing a hit); *fightCost* subtracts the heal of enemies still alive and kills healers first, like `healAndShoot` (heal-blind, a pair "took" two M3R3 + M4H2 for 1227 hits and both waves died); *towers change movement* — cohesion gathers at the coverage edge (tight gap near a tower, no hold inside coverage: five held for laggards 14 cells from the tower for 70 ticks losing one per shot), `inCombat` means enemy creeps only (the full cell score under a tower alone gave ally-influence inertia: ten fighters stood at the swamp edge for 120 ticks), the swamp-pinning term excludes tower fire (standing still costs the same), the tower's sustained damage is a tie-breaker in every cell score so the ring forms on the far side, an armed striker in an active siege does not flee the next shot (its death is already priced in and it soaks a shot a healthy one would take), and `shoot` targets the spawn under storm only while no combat creep is in range (haulers feeding the tower must not draw mass attacks); *unseen damage* — each fighter remembers its hits and cell; a loss that neither creep fire nor a tower burst explains is logged as `ghost damage`, counts as under fire and forces a retreat when one more such hit would kill; *diagnostics* — `tower:` at hello prints the constants and the model's shots at 1..4/10/20, `enemy structures` (every 50 ticks) prints towers with energy/cooldown/fed, enemy ramparts and construction sites with progress. Stub: `tower` scenario (a tower adjacent to the enemy spawn with four M1C1 feeders drawing from the enemy spawn) won at 909 by a single wave of six, `tower+enemy` at 946 by seven; none 461, enemy 508 and ball 1139 (heal-aware cost waits for one more fighter), swamp swarm 469, raider 524, freeze 573, errors 0.

**Fourteenth version (after the twelfth match, 03.09 — lost; a sixth opponent: two `M5R1` (400 each, full speed on swamp) spawned at ticks 1 and 20, a third `M3R1` at 220, no economy at all; they hunted our only hauler, kited the breacher to death, ate two runt fighters one at a time and sieged the spawn from range 3 with 30 dps while our spawn held 262 energy):** the loss was sealed at tick 0 — the breacher-first opening spends the initial 1000 before the enemy's opening is visible, and against fast harassers there is no recovery from 262 energy and one hauler. Now: *intel from the enemy spawn* — a creep the enemy orders at tick 1 is visible as `spawning` from tick 2 (`pendingEnemies`, logged as `enemy spawning` with `enemySpawn.spawning.remainingTime/needTime` when the API exposes it); pending combat creeps count as approaching (arrival = remaining spawn ticks + the walk), feed the production estimate and the spawn's threat power; *the opening waits to see it* — no spawn order until the enemy's first creep is visible or half the approach window (10 ticks) has passed, whichever is first (a real match resolves at tick 2; only the synthetic stub with pre-placed static guards needed the 10); *arrival by body* — a new enemy's arrival is its real walk down the approach field with `periodAt` per cell (`pathTicks`; the swamp-weighted field said 300+ for an M5R1 that walks swamp at one cell per tick); *guard first when the breach cannot pay in time* — `breachGuardReady` = breacher spawn (if none yet) + remaining wall hits / its dps + walk to the container + a full body at `targetIncome` + its spawn time (≈170 here); a threat arriving earlier makes the spawn build the full fighter from the initial energy (`fighterFirst`, income floored by the measured spawn regen so a zero-hauler start no longer reads as "never") and postpone the breacher; the breacher itself is built only at its optimal size — when the energy for it is not already in the spawn or on the way, haulers get the turn (after a guard-first opening the old "save for it" starved the economy for 300 ticks); *no runt that changes nothing* — under a deficit a body smaller than the full one is built only if it closes the deficit by itself (`closesDeficit`, Lanchester with enemy heal) or the spawn is already under fire; *power without fictional hits* — a unit's hits enter Lanchester weighted by the share of its damage that reaches the opponent (`weightedHits`: a kited melee counts a tenth), so the breacher's 1200 hits no longer make the home fight "winnable" while it lands nothing; *melee on a leash by catchability* — the breacher goes after a home threat only if it can catch it (melee target, within 2, immobile, or slower on swamp), otherwise it keeps the post and lunges when the kiter comes within 2; *ghost damage* is explained by last tick's shooters at last tick's positions (a shooter killed in the same tick as its last shot is no longer in the enemy list), the log line carries the tick and the nearest combat enemy. Stub gained `harass` (two M5R1 via the enemy spawn at tick 1, an M3R1 at 220; hunt haulers, kite fighters, shoot the spawn from range 3): the guard M11R3 is ordered at tick 2, kills both raiders by 400, the spawn dies at 904; tower 905, tower+enemy 951, none 428, enemy 463, swarm 435, raider 502, ball 1140, freeze 584, errors 0, ghost lines 0.

**Fifteenth version (after the thirteenth match, 03.09 — lost to the tower opponent again; the `enemy spawning` intel worked from tick 2 (the API does expose spawning enemy creeps and `spawn.spawning.remainingTime/needTime`), the breach and the economy ran fine, the tower was built at tick ~360, and from tick 1090 nine fresh M8R4 died one at a time to a ball of two `M3R3` + two `M4H2` that hovered 6-12 cells from our spawn for 300 ticks; the log ends at 1800 with no fighters and thirteen hauler husks):** four mechanisms, all found in the code, none of them a number. *Healers were "soft" targets* — `combatEnemies` required melee or ranged dps, so an `M4H2` was classified with the haulers: fighters chased it as a *raider* (no strength check at all) straight into its escort of four `M3R3` (t=1150), the hunt pack and the local Lanchester excluded it (so its 48 heal/tick was invisible: a pair walked into a ball with three healers at t=1060), and the home threat list ignored it; now a creep with melee, ranged **or heal** is army (the alarm and `spawnUnderFire` still count only dps: a lone healer near the spawn must not force fighter-first). *The cell score pulled non-aggressive fighters into the ball* — `scoreCell` valued outgoing fire at 30 per mass-attack unit (10 per adjacent enemy) regardless of the aggression verdict, against 0.3 per incoming hp: a fresh fighter holding the post saw +840 for a cell inside the ball and −18 for the damage there, walked in with `aggr=false`, and nine did so in a row; now without local superiority the outgoing term counts only on cells with zero net incoming damage (a free shot at slow melee stays free; a trade in a lost fight is worth nothing — `shoot` fires at everything in range regardless, this is movement only). *"At the gates" overrode the strength test* — `homeAtGates` (home threat within post + range + 1 = 7 cells) made everyone fight regardless of ratio, so each time the ball stepped to 6-7 cells the one healthy fighter charged (the other four were weaponless husks, the breacher cannot catch kiters), the ball stepped back, `homeFight` flipped off, and the fighter was left inside it; now "at the gates" means *inside the post* (within `HOME_STANDOFF`), `spawnUnderFire` keeps the match-9 rule, `homeWins` has hysteresis (0.9 to continue a fight, 1.2 to start it) and is scored against the *whole pack around the home threats* (enemies within 11 of any of them — the 40-tick alarm ring used to cut a ball on swamp in two and rate "the garrison wins" against three of eight); a weaker post now contracts into the spawn's shadow (the damage term is the only mover) and the enemy can reach the spawn only through the whole squad at once. *The siege simulation ignored a tower under construction* — wave 1 left at tick 327 against a site at 945/1250 and was recalled 35 ticks later when the tower stood; now enemy construction sites whose `progressTotal` equals the tower's build cost (the API hides `structure` on enemy sites) become `PendingTower`s with an ETA from the observed build rate (after half the approach window) or, before that, from `BUILD_POWER × WORK` of enemy creeps within 3 of the site; a site that will complete before the wave's arrival + siege is a fed tower in `siegeOutcome` (`TowerInfo` now carries a position and an optional structure so sites and towers share the code). Diagnostics: the `t=` line prints `home=fight:fire|gates|wins(ours/theirs)` or `hold(ours/theirs)` and `towers=fed/built+pending`; `enemy structures` prints `pendingTowers: (x,y)eta=…`. Stub gained `towersite` (a tower site at the enemy spawn from tick 150 built at 5/tick by an `M1C1W1` — no wave leaves before the tower stands, won at 911), `healball` (the enemy spawn — given 15 energy/tick, as its ten haulers gave it in the match — orders `M3R3`/`M4H2` alternately every 60 ticks from 280, groups of four rally and advance as a ball that hovers 6 cells from our spawn, shoots the nearest of ours within 3, backs off from a fighter within 2, heals the most damaged mate, hunts haulers within 8; alone it is out-rushed at 460, with the tower it is beaten at 1355) and `hover` (four `M3R3` + four `M4H2` placed 9 cells from our spawn at tick 500 with the same AI: with the tower, `home=hold` for 150 ticks while the sixth and seventh fighter are built, then `fight:wins(1088/758)`, the ball dies for one fighter, spawn at 1098 — before the pack fix the same run read `fight:wins(635/464)` on the first tick). Regression: none 428, enemy 464, swarm 435, raider 502, harass 904, tower 905, tower+enemy 951, freeze 584; ball 1346 (was 1140: non-aggressive fighters no longer trade with a faster melee ball they are rated to lose against — the stub's ball ignores them, so that trade used to be free there), errors 0, ghost lines 0. Not addressed: five haulers died walking past the ball parked on the chokepoint north of our pocket (their routes to the northern sites pass within its range).

**Sixteenth version (after the fourteenth match, 03.09)**: lost to an M5R1 rusher (two from tick 1, a third at ~200; they parked within three cells of our spawn and never kited) on the same map as matches 12–13. Five defects, all algorithmic: (1) `breachGuardReady` added the break time to `1000 / targetIncome()` — accumulation at the TARGET income (27/tick) right after the breach, while the real fleet at that moment was one 200-cost hauler at ~5/tick that had gone to a corner container 45 cells away; it said "guard by 167" against an enemy arrival of 168 and the breacher went first; at tick 167 the spawn held 278 energy and no shooter. It is now a tick-by-tick simulation of the spawner's own policy from the current state: breacher (if none) out of the spawn energy, the breach container joins the fleet's sites at `breachOpenIn` (breacher's walk along the flow field + remaining wall hits / live ATTACK), haulers are bought while it is their turn (`HAULER_LEAD`), there is something to haul and the projected income is below target, the rest is saved for a full body; horizon = the ticks left in the match. Stub check: freeze predicted the full fighter 226 ticks after the breacher, the real one came at ~218; none predicted 197, real ~185. (2) The regen sample on tick 1 read a full spawn (1000/1000, d = 0) and gave regen 0.0 until the second sample, so the simulation did not grow by a single unit — a full spawn is no longer sampled. (3) The spawn branch sized the breacher from the energy minus a minimal hauler (6 blocks, 780) while the estimate used the full energy (7 blocks, 910): `plannedBreacherBlocks` is the one body for the branch, `breachOpenIn` and the simulation. (4) Hauler #1 went to the corner (45 cells, a 90-tick trip) while the breach nine cells away opened 50 ticks later: the locked breach container is now a site candidate with trip = max(steps to the wall, `breachOpenIn`) + loaded ticks back along the Dijkstra path (`BreachPlan.steps/loadedTicks`); the hauler waits next to the wall (`TO_BREACH`) holding the container id as its sticky site and loads the tick the wall falls (live-map stub: h14 waits at (12,49)). (5) The M6A6 breacher stood 100 ticks at range 3 from two stationary M5R1 shooting it and the spawn and never struck: `catchable` said "faster shooter, cannot catch", and the melee's cell score sees a strike only from an adjacent cell (one-step greedy). `catchable` now also holds when the target is NOT retreating (its cell of the previous tick, `enemyPrevCell`, was not closer to us than its current one): the melee walks at a standing shooter with the enemy as its target (standoff 1, flow gradient), the moment the shooter steps away it is uncatchable again and the melee returns to the post; the melee's home target is the nearest catchable threat, not the earliest-arriving one. Stub `camp` (two M5R1 at range 3 from the breacher, never kiting): both dead within 12 ticks. Diagnostics: the `spawn: breacher` line prints `trip/open/sim/arrival` plus a 50-tick trace of the simulation (`t:e=/f=/i=/trip=/h=/sp=`); haulers print `TO_BREACH`. Stub results (v16): none 428, enemy 464, swarm 435, raider 502, harass 904, tower 905, tower+enemy 951, ball 1346, tower+healball 1134 (was 1355), towersite 911, healball 460, tower+hover 942 (was 1098), freeze 575, rush 825, camp 430, live-map rush (the match-14 replay) 929 with our spawn losing 90 hits; errors 0, ghost lines 0. Not addressed: after a fighter-first opening the economy runs on regen plus the far corners for ~200 ticks (the first hauler at t≈200); unescorted haulers still die to kiters.

**Seventeenth version (after the fifteenth match, 03.09)**: lost to the tower opponent again (ten M1C1 haulers, a tower at (7,50) from t≈361, then M3R3/M4H2 in a stream that accelerated to one creep per 20 ticks after their own breach opened at t≈830). The opening and the breach worked as the stub predicted: guard by tick 226 predicted, the first full fighter born at 209, hauler #1 waited at the wall and loaded the tick it fell, income 32/tick by t=210. The loss came from four algorithmic defects: (1) The hunt counted only the pack within 11 cells of the threat: five fighters (two crippled) went for two (cost 265 vs slack 900), the enemy stream added two more during the 60-tick approach and cohesion wait, and at contact the four-pack cost 983 — the local decision flipped to non-aggressive, the fighters retreated through swamp with fatigue 24–32 under fire and lost two without a kill (t=960–1060). The hunt pack now also includes every combat enemy whose path time to the threat (its own body along the flow field to the threat) is no longer than our strikers' path time — what will be there when we arrive. (2) `fightCost ≤ speedSlack` gated the fight even in contact; retreating from a shooter who is already in range is the same exchange without the kills. In contact (any enemy within RANGED_RANGE+1 of any local ally) the Lanchester ratio alone decides, with its 1.3/0.9 hysteresis; the cost gate only decides whether to ENTER a fight (hunt and local alike). (3) The spawn kept buying haulers while `projectedIncome < target` although twelve haulers idled at 450 energy on the ground (haulers #13–#15 at t=680–750, 1500 energy = 1.5 fighters). Fleet income is now bounded by the energy the fleet can actually haul over FLEET_ROUNDS (`fleetHaul` returns trip and energy taken; `incomeOf` = min(capacity, taken/rounds)/trip), and a hauler is bought only when capacity is the bottleneck (`capacityBound`: a fleet enlarged by one minimal hauler would haul more over the rounds than the current one). The breach-guard simulation uses the same model. (4) A wave member that lost all RANGED walked home (the `!hasWeapon` branch) but stayed in the wave, so cohesion held the whole wave for it — seven fighters stood 40 cells from the enemy spawn for a hundred ticks (stub tower+stream, spawn dead at 1545 instead of 1125; the same pattern existed in v16 with 27 such lines). Weaponless fighters are dropped from the wave. Diagnostics: `spawn: fighter` prints `our/enemy deficit fire arrival`; `posture:` prints every 100 ticks, not only on change. Stub: new `stream` scenario (the match-15 opponent: M3R3/M4H2 alternating every 40 ticks from t=280, each walking to our spawn alone; combine with `tower`). Results (v17): none 428, enemy 464, swarm 435, raider 502, harass 904, tower 884 (was 905), tower+enemy 921 (951), ball 1150 (1346), tower+healball 983 (1134), towersite 888 (911), healball 460, tower+hover 942, rush 825, camp 430, stream 473, tower+stream 1092 (v16 1125; fifteen fighters bought, fifteen alive at the end vs thirteen), freeze 575, live-map rush 929; errors 0, ghost lines 0. Not addressed: the enemy's late-game economy (their own breach) outgrew ours from t≈850 while we never had the seven fighters the siege simulation needs against a fed tower with ten feeders; the unexplained 450-cost M6R1 built under alarm at t=1211 (the camp rule should have held it — the new spawn diagnostics will show why next time).

**Eighteenth version (after the sixteenth match, 03.09)**: the sixteenth match (v17, the tower opponent of matches 11/13/15 again) was won at the very end — the log ends at tick 1980 with the enemy spawn at 336 hits and seven shooters in range — but only by the last-call push at 1754. The v17 mechanisms held up live: hunts went only for singles and pairs and killed four stream creeps without a loss, the crippled fighter dropped out of the wave at 950 and walked home alone, the fleet stopped at eleven haulers and none were bought while the fleet idled, every fighter was a full 1000 body. The failure: the first wave of seven departed at 824, marched 200 ticks, fought its way to twenty cells from the enemy spawn, and at 1035 the continuation siege (siegeGo) flipped to lose after a skirmish with a three-pack — so the wave was recalled and walked 190 cells home through the stream with the pack on its heels: one fighter dead, four crippled, the enemy spawn untouched. Two structural changes: (1) A wave in the field is no longer recalled when the siege stops adding up (home threats still recall it, as before). It holds at the tower's edge instead — `siegeHold`: fighters inside a fed tower's reach step out (`towerEdgeStep`), fighters outside never step into it, contact enemies are fought by the local score; and a `siegeJoin` simulation (front + staging) sends the staging group to join the front when the combined siege wins. The continuation siege is computed on the wave FRONT (members within the marching cohesion gap of the vanguard along the flow to the enemy spawn), not on every departed member, so reinforcements a hundred cells behind neither inflate the verdict nor get waited for; cohesion across waves applies only while holding. The hold applies only while the vanguard is outside tower coverage — inside, the wave is committed: leaving costs the same two shots as finishing, and an early draft that held mid-siege had survivors stepping out and re-entering one by one with the enemy spawn stuck at 668 hits for 300 ticks (stub tower+stream 1353). (2) On the march the local group is the wave within ENGAGE_RANGE, not neighbours within four cells: in the two-cell-wide corridor the wave moved as a column and its tail of two rated itself weaker than the three-pack and retreated while four marched ahead. Stub (v18): ball 807 (was 1150 — the waves are no longer recalled when the melee ball appears and win the spawn race), tower+stream 1120 (1092), everything else identical to v17: none 428, enemy 464, swarm 435, raider 502, harass 904, tower 884, tower+enemy 921, tower+healball 983, towersite 888, healball 460, tower+hover 942, rush 825, camp 430, stream 473, freeze 575, live-map rush 929; errors 0, ghost lines 0. `posture:` now prints `join=` `hold=` `front=a/b`; `push=true(hold)` marks a holding wave. Not addressed: the siege under a fed tower still stalls once the first wave's survivors flee the burst (tower+stream: spawn at 1028 for a hundred ticks until wave 2), and our late economy on far temporary containers (income 4–9) stayed far below the enemy's.

**Nineteenth version** (match 17, 04.09.2026, lost on the match-14 map to an M5R1 opener plus an M3R3/M4H2 stream fed by ten M1C1 haulers, ~7 energy/tick): the fighter-first opening killed the rusher without loss, then the spawn deadlocked — `fighterFirst` held energy for a 1000-cost fighter at 1 energy/tick while forbidding the haulers that alone could raise the flow (t=210–430: no hauler, no fighter, "guard by breach in 495"), the breach never opened (the breacher waited for its 980 optimum while haulers ate every 200), income stayed 1–3 against the enemy's ~7, and one damaged fighter met M3R3+M4H2 at t=600. Fixes, all computed from state: (1) fighter-first is a comparison, not a flag — `holdReady` (a full fighter from what is in the spawn and en route at the current flow, buying nothing) against `investReady` (`guardReadySim`: the spawner's own policy run forward — breacher, haulers, then the fighter out of the grown income); hold only when that fighter arrives in time for the threat, or sooner than investing, or an affordable undersized body closes the deficit; otherwise invest. Alarm goes through the same comparison with the threat horizon capped at `SPAWN_ALARM_TICKS` (a parked ball has `enemyArrival` = never; without the cap tower+hover bought four haulers under alarm). (2) The breacher body is sized for the current energy flow (`breacherBlocksFor`: accumulation + spawn + walk + break, not √(H/180) with free energy); the plan's objective is the **income start** — wall open AND a minimum hauler at the container (`breachIncomeStart`; without the hauler in the objective the wall opened at 350 and the first hauler arrived at 470); idle ranged fighters' fire on the wall counts (`wallFire` — the post fighter took 3060 hits off the wall in a hundred ticks); the breacher is bought only if it moves the income start earlier by more than its cost (`breacherOrderOf`: gain = income delta × ticks saved, capped by the container's energy), and a minimum hauler goes first when the breacher becomes affordable sooner through its income. (3) `guardReadySim` mirrors those rules and also runs without a breach. Diagnostics: `spawn: fighter first — enemy arrives in … hold= invest= deficit= alarm= closes= flow=`, `spawn: saving for breacher blocks= cost= …`, `spawn: breacher … fire= open= hold= invest= sim=`. Stub: new live-map scenario `stream17` (the match-17 opponent: an M5R1 camper at t=1, then the match's spawn schedule walking in pairs under the healball rules, +7/tick enemy income) — v18 loses its spawn at 1044, v19 kills the enemy spawn at 835; `rush` 708 (825), everything else unchanged; errors 0, ghost 0. Not addressed: a lone fighter engages a healer pair it cannot out-damage once the spawn is under fire (`fight:fire` ignores the score); the fighter body is chosen without regard to enemy healing (M11R3's 30 dps against 24 heal/tick); nobody hunts the enemy's unescorted M1C1 haulers on their 45-cell corner runs. **Engine facts** (World engine `src/processor/intents/movement.js`; the operator confirms Arena uses the same movement rules): swaps and chains are legal — a cell is an obstacle only if its creep is not moving this tick, and a head-on pair gets top priority in conflicts; fatigue per move = (non-MOVE, non-CARRY parts **by type, dead ones included** + loaded CARRY parts, counted from the back) × 2 on plain / 10 on swamp, minus 2 per **live** MOVE each tick (the reduction happens in the same tick as the move, so a full M5R5 shows 0 on plain and 40 on swamp); a creep with fatigue > 0 at intent time does not move. A loaded C5M5 hauler or a fresh M5R5 fighter therefore takes 5 ticks per swamp cell, and a damaged fighter is slower everywhere — waves must gather before contact, not during it, and bodies need spare MOVEs to survive damage at speed. **Arena tower** (arena.screeps.com/docs, verified against match 11 to the hit point): attack 1000 at range 1, −50 per cell, 0 from range 21; heal 600 −30 per cell; one action per 10-tick cooldown, 10 energy per shot, capacity 10 (one shot per refill); 3000 hits; construction cost 1250 at `BUILD_POWER` 5; buildable in Spawn and Swamp (the opponent did). It is nothing like the World tower (150/shot, capacity 50) — never reuse World tower numbers here.

**Twentieth version** (match 18, 04.09.2026, a draw against けろびー at the tick limit — the twentieth match of a series that otherwise went 19-0): the enemy spawn stood at 3000 hits from the first tick to the last, while our side finished with 22 fighters and a power score of 3498 against nothing. The clock beat us, not the opponent. Its tower went up at t=410; raids kept our army near zero until t=1200, then stopped; from 1200 to 1476 we sat at home with a growing army and no enemy in the field, because the siege simulation kept saying "lose" against the tower; the first wave left at 1476 with 524 ticks left and 212 of travel, reached the tower at ~1690, `siegeGo` flipped to "lose", `hold` came on at 1800, and reinforcements dribbled out one fighter at a time (waves 4, 5 and 6 were single creeps). The last call fired at 1990 — it measured travel by the *nearest* fighter (51 cells) and damage by the *whole* army including everyone at home, so it never noticed that the mass of the army was a hundred cells away. Three rules, all computed from state: (1) a **departure deadline** — the attack only fits if the group still has time to walk and finish, so the need is `travelOf(group) + siege.ticks` where `travelOf` is the distance of the group's *furthest* member (they walk together, and the siege starts when the last one arrives) and the siege duration comes from the simulation itself, capped at `SIEGE_LIMIT`; past the deadline we go with whatever verdict we have, because a draw is worth exactly as much as a defeat. (2) The last call may now go with a losing siege **when home has nothing left to lose** — `homeAtRisk` asks whether the enemy's nearest fighter can still reach our spawn and chew 3000 hits in the ticks that remain (a creep born later cannot cross the map); while it can, the army stays, which is the rule from match 9 that kept a lost army from turning a draw into a defeat. (3) **Holding the tower edge got a clock**: `holdInTime` requires that the reinforcement (from the staging post, or from the spawn when the post is empty) can still arrive and finish the joint siege — holding for a group that cannot come is a draw on schedule. Departures on the last call also go as a group: a single fighter sent forty ticks before the end arrives nowhere, and at home he is a defender, so a lone reinforcement leaves only when there is already a wave ahead to join. Diagnostics: the `posture` line gained `need=<deadline budget>/<remaining>`, `risk=<homeAtRisk>` and `hold=false(late)` when the clock refused a hold. Stub: a new `fortress` scenario (three fed towers at the enemy spawn, eight feeders, a stream that dries up) plus `TICKS_LIMIT` in the stub's `arenaInfo`, which is what tests a deadline without tuning the opponent to produce one — on the same world with a 1100-tick clock the old version leaves at 979 and never touches the spawn (3000 hits, a draw), the new one leaves at 875 with ten fighters and takes it down to 760. At the full 2000-tick clock every earlier scenario is unchanged to the tick (`towersite` 888→882), `fortress` falls at 1534, errors 0, ghost 0.

**Twenty-first version** (match 19, 05.09.2026, lost at t=1000 with 904 energy in the bank and not a single creep alive — the seventh match of a v20 series that had gone 6-0): the opponent hunted the haulers until income fell to zero, our fighters died one by one from t=900, and from t=850 to the end the spawn accumulated 584→904 energy and **built nothing at all** while it was being shot. Two waiting rules held it, and both were blind in the same way: the camp rule ("a stronger enemy is at the gates, save for a full body instead of feeding him one 300-cost creep at a time", from a match on 02.09 where eight undersized fighters died in a row) and the better-body rule, which waits while `enemyArrival > waitTicks`. An enemy who is **already standing next to the spawn and shooting it never "arrives"** — the log shows `arrival=-` and `arrival=4300` while the spawn burned — so both rules compared the wait against a threat that was, by their arithmetic, infinitely far away. The fix gives both of them the spawn's own clock: `spawnLife` = the spawn's hits divided by the incoming fire at its cell (`InfluenceMap.fireAt`), infinite when nothing is shooting; the camp rule now saves only while `spawnLife > waitTicks`, and the better-body rule returns only when *both* the enemy and the clock allow the wait. Verified by a fixture rather than by a tuned match: `run3.mjs <ticks> camped19` puts the bot in exactly the losing state — two M3R3 next to our spawn, no creeps of ours, 900 energy, no income, so the spawn has ~50 ticks to live and the last 100 energy would take 100 ticks to arrive. The version in `main` builds nothing and dies with 950 in the bank; this one immediately builds a 12-part fighter for 900 (`fire=true arrival=-`) and the creep is alive at the end. `regress.sh` gates that fixture on "built at least one body under fire". Every other scenario is unchanged to the tick. Not fixed: the reason the income died in the first place — the opponent's hunters killed haulers faster than we replaced them, and a fleet that is being hunted still routes by trip time alone.

**Twenty-second version** (match 20, 05.09.2026, lost at t=600 — the first match of a v21 series, which had just gone 12-0): at t=290 two fighters left on a `siegeStart` verdict of `win/208t` while the opponent's field army stood at 587 against our 324, a hundred and twenty ticks from our spawn. Reinforcements followed one at a time (waves 2 and 3 were single creeps, sent by the join path), the wave was shredded in the open — the survivors end the log at 300 hits out of 1200 — and the enemy walked into an empty house: 1084 against 301 at home, spawn down at 600. The departure gate asked two questions, and both were about the wave: can it kill the spawn (`siegeStart`), and does it survive the packs on the way (`attrition`, `waveAfter`). Nobody asked the third: **who holds the house while it is away.** `guardHolds` looked only at enemies already inside the 40-tick alarm ring, and an army two minutes out is not in it. Now the sortie has a window — `sortieTicks` = the group's own walk plus the siege duration its simulation predicts — and every approaching enemy whose `arrivalById` falls inside that window is counted against the garrison that stays (`homeGuard`, which already excludes the leavers); the wave departs only if that garrison holds them by `DEFEND_MARGIN`. The same condition guards the reinforcement path, which is how single creeps were leaking out to a holding wave. The `posture` line now prints `guardHolds=<ring>/<sortie>(<count>@<window>)`. The cost is tempo against opponents who keep a stream running, since their creeps are always inside somebody's window: `stream17` 835→1004, `tower+stream` 1120→1163, `tower+healball` 983→1014, `swarm` 435→455; the gain is in the fights that were being lost at home: `ball` 807→664, `fortress` 1534→1518. All 21 scenarios still end with the enemy spawn destroyed, errors 0. Not fixed: the siege simulation still calls a spawn "undefended" when the enemy army is out in the field rather than standing on it, so `siegeStart` will keep saying `win` in exactly the position where the sortie is suicidal — the new gate refuses the sortie, but the verdict that suggested it is still wrong.

**Twenty-third version** (match 22, 05.09.2026, lost at t=1000 to stachu3478 — read with `tools/replay.py`, which shows both sides' shots and heals and settled three questions our own logs could only guess at). What the replay said: **133 of his shots went into our unarmed haulers**, 132 of them with nothing armed of ours in reach — a deliberate hunt on the fleet; our income fell 22→4 while the fleet grew to eleven, half of all energy spent (4700 against 4780 on fighters). His fire was concentrated — 1.04 distinct targets per tick, focus share 0.98 — ours was not: 1.60 and 0.73, with 43 of our 74 aimed shots going into his healer while his ranged, in reach, was shot 41 % of the time and kept firing. His healers healed 1469 times adjacent; we healed zero. Three changes were tried, two kept: (1) **the fleet does not grow while it is not delivering what it projects** — `measureDelivery` counts the energy haulers actually hand to the spawn and `realisedIncome` averages it over `PRODUCTION_WINDOW`; a purchase requires the measured income to hold at `PUSH_RELEASE_RATIO` of the projection, so a hunted fleet (or one whose points decay before it arrives — the same symptom, and the reason the decay filter was once removed from the projection) stops growing. The projection stays as the target; the measurement is the veto. (2) **A fighter that cannot reach the global focus target now shoots what its neighbours can shoot** — the fallback ranks enemies by how many allied guns already bear on them, then by heal, then by hits, so neighbours converge without being told to. Stub: `tower` 884→838, `tower+enemy` 921→877, `tower+healball` 1014→965, `towersite` 882→839, `tower+stream` 1163→1015, `fortress` 1518→1381, the rest unchanged. (3) **HEAL parts in the fighter body — measured and rejected**, and the reasoning is in the code above `fighterBody`: both honest valuations (heal as hits returned over the fight, and heal as the survivability multiplier `enemyDps/(enemyDps − heal)`) are duel arithmetic, and both picked the same degenerate body — one gun and two heals, 10 damage against 24 heal. In a group fight three enemies shoot one target, the heal is diluted and the body has no damage left: `fortress` stopped being taken at all, `tower+stream` 1591 against 1015. His trick is not heal in the body — it is a **separate healer paired with a full-damage shooter**, a two-creep formation, and that is the next candidate.

**The healer formation — attempted twice and rejected, 05.09.2026.** Match 22's replay showed the opponent's pair — a full-damage `M5R5` beside a `T1M5H3` that healed 1469 times adjacent — holding the field while our fighters died one at a time, so the natural next step was to field the same pair. Two forms were built and measured. **(a) HEAL parts inside the fighter body** (see the note above `fighterBody`): every honest valuation of heal is duel arithmetic and picks one gun and two heals; `fortress` stopped being taken at all. **(b) A separate healer creep**: body `[MOVE×k, HEAL×k]` (M3H3 for 1000 — 36 heal, 600 hits, MOVE in front so the heal outlives the rest, exactly as the opponent builds it), chosen against a fighter by one measure — `armyScoreWith`, our Lanchester power over theirs, with our heal subtracted from their damage the same way `enemyPowerOf` already subtracts theirs from ours — plus the behaviour to go with it: the healer walks to the ward that is most damaged by fraction of hits (from the departed wave if there is one, so it marches with it), stands at range 1 where a heal is worth `HEAL_POWER` per part instead of a quarter of it, and does not flee an enemy that is next to its ward. The result was worse than the first attempt: the bot built **45 healers and one fighter**, and five scenarios stopped killing the enemy spawn at all (`enemy`, `tower+enemy`, `tower+healball`, `tower+stream`, `freeze`; `swarm` 455→877, `tower+hover` 943→1936, `fortress` 1381→1985). The cause is not the body or the behaviour but **the power model**: heal enters it as a subtraction from the enemy's damage, so as our heal approaches his damage his power tends to zero and the score of one more healer is unbounded — the same degeneracy as in (a), one level up. What the model is missing is geometry. His pair works because his shooter fights at the edge of its range while the healer stands out of ours; our healer would walk into the same focused fire, and his focus is near-perfect (measured: 0.98 focus share, 1.04 distinct targets per tick). Valuing heal correctly needs a model of who can be reached by whose fire, which our power function does not have; adding heal to it without that model produces an unbounded number, and both attempts found it. Recorded as a limitation only after both removals were built, run and measured.

**Twenty-fourth version — the bot learns to build, 05.09.2026 (matches 24 draw and 25 lost, both against the two players above us).** Reading those two logs answered the question the previous three versions kept circling. It is not that our thresholds are wrong: **we convert energy only into creeps, and the top two convert part of theirs into structures.** In match 24 stachu3478 put a `1250` site next to his spawn at t=150, finished the tower by t=360 and added two ramparts at t=400; our wave reached thirty-two ticks from that spawn and the match ended a draw. In match 25 けろびー built three `1000` sites in a row in mid-map — `(52,34)`, `(56,56)`, `(37,49)`, each about a hundred ticks apart, each next to a container his `M2C2W2` was standing on; `1000` is the cost of `StructureSpawn` and of nothing else in `CONSTRUCTION_COST`, so those were forward spawns. We had built nothing in twenty-five matches.

What the arena actually offers, read out of the client's own typings rather than guessed: a tower is `1250` for 1000 damage at range 1 falling 50 a cell, cooldown 10, three thousand hits; a rampart is `200` for **ten thousand hits** and is impassable to the opponent; a wall is `100` for the same hits and impassable to everyone; an extension is `200` for `100` more spawn capacity; a road is `10`, or `50` on swamp, and turns a loaded hauler's five ticks per swamp cell into one. And the constraint that shapes all of it: **the Arena `Creep` has `build` and no `repair` and no `dismantle`** — structures are placed at full hits and can never be topped up, which is why the rampart's ten thousand matter and why nobody rebuilds a broken tower.

This version builds the tower, and only the tower. A **keeper** (`[MOVE×2, CARRY×2, WORK×k]`, `WORK` last so damage takes the mobility before the trade) carries energy from the spawn into the site and afterwards keeps a shot in the tower — its capacity is one shot, so a tower without a keeper is silent. The size of `k` is not chosen but solved: total time is accumulation at the current flow plus `1250 / (BUILD_POWER × k)`, and its minimum is `k = √(price × flow / (BUILD_POWER × cost(WORK)))` — four or five at the flows we see. The tower's cell is the second ring around the spawn: closer and it takes a cell newborn creeps come out on, further and it loses fifty damage a cell and leaves no cell from which the keeper reaches both spawn and tower; among the second-ring cells it takes the one nearest the enemy spawn, because that is the side they arrive from. It fires at whoever it can kill with this shot (a shot does not divide), else at the most dangerous creep in reach, and when there is nothing to shoot it heals our most damaged creep — six hundred a shot, fifty `HEAL` parts.

**The purchase is decided by the same yardstick as everything else, and the two terms it needed are the finding.** The comparison is the *marginal* gain in `ourPowerOf` against **the enemies actually present**, with their heal subtracted (`lanchester`): against a pair whose healer restores 36 a tick, a fighter's continuous 50 is nearly cancelled while the tower's thousand-in-one-shot is not, and that difference is invisible if the tower is valued against an abstract body instead of the enemy in front of it. Then two corrections, both measured, not assumed: the tower only works at home, so its damage and hits are multiplied by `homeShare()` — the share of the last `PRODUCTION_WINDOW` ticks under alarm; and a fighter is a consumable, so its gain is multiplied by `survivalOfFighters()` — the cost of the still-living parts of all our fighters over everything spent on fighters. Without that second term the model says a fighter is always the better buy, which is true right up to the moment our fighters start dying. The trace is printed (`tower: worth=… share=… surv=… tower=…/price fighter=…/price`), and on the stub it reads: at 33 % home and nothing lost yet, fighter 205 against tower 81; at 50 % and nothing lost, 204 against 120; at 67 % home with a quarter of the fighter investment dead, tower 169 against fighter 152 — and it builds.

**Geometry entered the power model here, in the one place it can be stated exactly.** `ourTowerDps` adds our towers' fire to `ourPowerOf` and `closesDeficit` by *real range* — the shot falls fifty a cell and past `TOWER_FALLOFF_RANGE` it is nothing, so a home tower contributes exactly zero to a siege at the enemy's spawn and almost its full damage against a camp at our gates. The tower's three thousand hits are deliberately **not** added to the group's hit pool there: they are our resource only while the enemy is shooting the tower, and a group's hits in Lanchester mean hits that absorb focus fire. (In the purchase comparison they do count, weighted by `homeShare`, on the reasoning that an enemy committed to our spawn cannot both ignore the tower and take the house.)

New stub scenario **`pairs`** — the opponent we actually lose to, which the harness did not have: `M5R5` and `M5H3` alternating every ninety ticks from t=250, one group per pair so the healer heals its shooter adjacent, and **no kiting** — they walk to our spawn and stand there trading our damage against their heal. Every other ranged opponent in the harness retreats from a fighter at two cells; this one does not, and that is what made matches 24 and 25 different from everything the stub could produce. Both `pairs` and `tower+pairs` are in the gate.
